        <script>
        function createCookie(name, value, expires) {
            document.cookie = name + "=" + value + "; max-age=" + expires + "; path=/";
        }
        function checkCookie(name) {
            const nameEQ = name + "=";
            const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
            for (let i = 0; i < ca.length; i = i + 1) {
                let c = ca[i];
                while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
                if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
            }
            return null;
        }
        // Initialize consent mode
        window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
        function gtag() { dataLayer.push(arguments); }

        // Check if user has already made a consent choice
        const consentCookie = checkCookie('user_consent');

        if (consentCookie) {
            // User has previously consented - parse their choices
            const consent = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(consentCookie));
            gtag('consent', 'default', consent);
        } else {
            // First visit - set default to denied
            gtag('consent', 'default', {
                'ad_user_data': 'denied',
                'ad_personalization': 'denied',
                'ad_storage': 'denied',
                'analytics_storage': 'denied',
                'wait_for_update': 500
            });
        }
    </script>

<div class="text-block post-block link-underline style-shaded">
    <h2 class="editorH2">Premier League To Be Relegated 2026/27 Odds</h2>

<p>Premier League relegation odds are never too kind on newly-promoted teams, but there are many other things to consider before consigning a side to the drop. New managers, new signings and Premier League experience count for a lot in the battle for survival.</p>

<p>The newly promoted trio of Coventry City, Hull City, and Ipswich Town are predicted&nbsp;to be up&nbsp;against it in 2026/27, with bookmakers handing the new boys short odds in the relegation market.</p>

<p>Sunderland, Leeds, Fulham, and Crystal Palace also feature in the ante-post odds.</p>

</div>
<div class="text-block post-block link-underline ">
    <h2 class="editorH2">Premier League Relegation Betting Markets</h2>

<p>All bookmakers will offer Premier League relegation odds but selecting one team to go down isn&rsquo;t the only way you can bet. Here are some of the other popular relegation betting markets.</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>To Be Relegated</strong></h3>

<p>Simply select one side to be relegated in this market. The three new boys - Hull, Coventry, and Ipswich - are the three ante-post favourite here.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>Relegation Treble</strong></h3>

<p>Taking things further is the relegation treble market, in which bettors wager on all three sides to go down. It&rsquo;s a bit like a mini accumulator: for the bet to be successful, all three selections must be relegated.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>To Finish Bottom</strong></h3>

<p>Who do you think will finish bottom of the Premier League table? Have your say here.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>Number of Promoted Teams Relegated</strong></h3>

<p>Many promoted clubs overperform in their first season in the Premier League, buoyed by the prospect of playing against some of the best players in the world. Yet this trend has ended with all three promoted clubs relegated in back-to-back seasons.</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>To Stay Up</strong></h3>

<p>You might have a hunch that one of the bookies&rsquo; favourites for relegation will outperform the gloomy predictions &ndash; you can wager on it in this market.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>Winner/To Finish Bottom</strong></h3>

<p><a href="https://www.bettingodds.com/betting-sites/football">Premier League betting sites</a> also take double wagers on the teams to finish top and bottom in the season. You can make your predictions at both ends in this market.</p>

<p>We&rsquo;ll be providing analysis and reviews of every weekend of Premier League action throughout the season. 	<li><strong>Compare Odds &mdash; </strong>Using our comparison grid, you can easily see which bookmakers offer the most competitive odds on the markets you&rsquo;re looking to wager in.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Research &mdash; </strong>Understanding what&rsquo;s going on before a match is critical if you want to place an informed bet. Take a look at recent player and team form, and whether there are any key injuries.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Know Your Limits &mdash; </strong>All UKGC-licensed bookmakers have limiters in place on their site that can be set by budget or time. Make use of them, never bet when inebriated, and don&rsquo;t chase losses. Remember that gambling is supposed to be fun and that you should only bet what you&rsquo;re comfortable losing.&nbsp;</li>

    <h2 class="editorH2"><strong>Premier League Relegation History</strong></h2>

<p>Since the 95/96 season, three clubs have been relegated from the Premier League every year.</p>

<p>In the past managers have referred to the &ldquo;magic 40&rdquo; in regard to how many points are generally required to guarantee survival in a 38-game season, but the actual number can be higher or lower than that. Let&rsquo;s take a look at every team to be relegated from the Premier League, and the points total of the team finishing one place beneath the cut-off.&nbsp;</p>

    <h2 class="editorH2">Frequently Asked Questions on Premier League Relegation</h2>

<ul>
</ul>

</div>

    <div class="faq-container post-block" itemscope itemtype="https://schema.org/FAQPage">


                    <div class="faq-item" itemscope itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question">
                <h3 class="fi-question" itemprop="name">Where can I bet on Premier League relegation?</h3>
                <div class="fi-answer" itemscope itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer">
                    <div itemprop="text"><p>BettingOdds directs punters towards all the best Premier League betting sites, where you can bet on relegation. Browse the latest at the top of this page.</p>
</div>
                </div>
            </div>
                    <div class="faq-item" itemscope itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question">
                <h3 class="fi-question" itemprop="name">Which clubs have been relegated from the Premier League the most times?</h3>
                <div class="fi-answer" itemscope itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer">
                    <div itemprop="text"><p>Norwich have been <a href='https://www.guinnessworldrecords.com/world-records/most-times-relegated-from-the-english-football-premier-league/' rel='nofollow' target='_blank'>relegated from the Premier League the most times</a>, relegated five separate times from the top-flight thanks to their demotion last term, while Crystal Palace and Sunderland have both been relegated from the PL four times in their history.</p>

<ul>
	<li>Norwich&nbsp;- 6&nbsp;times</li>
	<li>West Brom, Burnley - 5 times</li>
	<li>Leicester, Middlesbrough, Sheffield United, Sunderland, Watford - 4 times</li>
</ul>
</div>
                </div>
            </div>
                    <div class="faq-item" itemscope itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question">
                <h3 class="fi-question" itemprop="name">Which betting site has the best Premier League relegation odds?</h3>
                <div class="fi-answer" itemscope itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer">
                    <div itemprop="text"><p>BOYLE Sports currently offers the best odds on Premier League relegation, but this can change throughout the course of the season.&nbsp;</p>
</div>
                </div>
            </div>
                    <div class="faq-item" itemscope itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question">
                <h3 class="fi-question" itemprop="name">Which teams were relegated from the Premier League in 2025/26?</h3>
                <div class="fi-answer" itemscope itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer">
                    <div itemprop="text"><p>Wolves, Burnley, and West Ham were relegated from the Premier League in 2025/26.</p>
</div>
                </div>
            </div>
                    <div class="faq-item" itemscope itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question">
                <h3 class="fi-question" itemprop="name">Who was promoted from the Championship in 2025/26?</h3>
                <div class="fi-answer" itemscope itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer">
